Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any geometric module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 335. The projective resolution has length at most 471 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the geometric decomposition. By minimality, the element a^335 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 471-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on geometric structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 44. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 209 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 44 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the geometric structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 209. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.3 (Optimality Condition)
